PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an expert at scheduling meetings. You are given a few constraints on the existing schedule of each participant, the meeting duration, and possibly some preferences on the meeting time. Note there exists a solution that works with existing schedule of every participant. Here are a few example tasks and solutions:

TASK: You need to schedule a meeting for Michelle, Steven and Jerry for one hour between the work hours of 9:00 to 17:00 on Monday. 

Here are the existing schedules for everyone during the day: 
Michelle has meetings on Monday during 11:00 to 12:00; 
Steven has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 11:30 to 12:00, 13:30 to 14:00, 15:30 to 16:00; 
Jerry has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 10:00 to 11:00, 11:30 to 12:30, 13:00 to 14:30, 15:30 to 16:00, 16:30 to 17:00; 

Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints. 
SOLUTION: 
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
You are an expert at scheduling meetings. You are given a few constraints on the existing schedule of each participant, the meeting duration, and possibly some preferences on the meeting time. Note there exists a solution that works with existing schedule of every participant. Here are a few example tasks and solutions:

TASK: You need to schedule a meeting for Michelle, Steven and Jerry for one hour between the work hours of 9:00 to 17:00 on Monday. 

Here are the existing schedules for everyone during the day: 
Michelle has meetings on Monday during 11:00 to 12:00; 
Steven has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 11:30 to 12:00, 13:30 to 14:00, 15:30 to 16:00; 
Jerry has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 10:00 to 11:00, 11:30 to 12:30, 13:00 to 14:30, 15:30 to 16:00, 16:30 to 17:00; 

Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints. 
SOLUTION: 
================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain meeting_scheduler)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types person time)
  (:constants
    michelle steven jerry - person
  )
  (:predicates
    (next ?t1 ?t2 - time)
    (free ?p - person ?t1 ?t2 - time)
    (meeting_scheduled)
  )
  (:action schedule_one_hour_meeting
    :parameters (?ts ?tm ?te - time)
    :precondition (and
      (next ?ts ?tm)
      (next ?tm ?te)
      (free michelle ?ts ?tm)
      (free michelle ?tm ?te)
      (free steven ?ts ?tm)
      (free steven ?tm ?te)
      (free jerry ?ts ?tm)
      (free jerry ?tm ?te)
    )
    :effect (and
      (meeting_scheduled)
    )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem schedule_monday_meeting)
  (:domain meeting_scheduler)
  (:objects
    t0900 t0930 t1000 t1030 t1100 t1130 t1200 t1230 t1300 t1330 t1400 t1430 t1500 t1530 t1600 t1630 t1700 - time
  )
  (:init
    ;; Timeline definition (30-minute intervals)
    (next t0900 t0930)
    (next t0930 t1000)
    (next t1000 t1030)
    (next t1030 t1100)
    (next t1100 t1130)
    (next t1130 t1200)
    (next t1200 t1230)
    (next t1230 t1300)
    (next t1300 t1330)
    (next t1330 t1400)
    (next t1400 t1430)
    (next t1430 t1500)
    (next t1500 t1530)
    (next t1530 t1600)
    (next t1600 t1630)
    (next t1630 t1700)

    ;; Michelle availability (Blocked 11:00-12:00)
    (free michelle t0900 t0930)
    (free michelle t0930 t1000)
    (free michelle t1000 t1030)
    (free michelle t1030 t1100)
    (free michelle t1200 t1230)
    (free michelle t1230 t1300)
    (free michelle t1300 t1330)
    (free michelle t1330 t1400)
    (free michelle t1400 t1430)
    (free michelle t1430 t1500)
    (free michelle t1500 t1530)
    (free michelle t1530 t1600)
    (free michelle t1600 t1630)
    (free michelle t1630 t1700)

    ;; Steven availability (Blocked 9:00-9:30, 11:30-12:00, 13:30-14:00, 15:30-16:00)
    (free steven t0930 t1000)
    (free steven t1000 t1030)
    (free steven t1030 t1100)
    (free steven t1100 t1130)
    (free steven t1200 t1230)
    (free steven t1230 t1300)
    (free steven t1300 t1330)
    (free steven t1400 t1430)
    (free steven t1430 t1500)
    (free steven t1500 t1530)
    (free steven t1600 t1630)
    (free steven t1630 t1700)

    ;; Jerry availability (Blocked 9:00-9:30, 10:00-11:00, 11:30-12:30, 13:00-14:30, 15:30-16:00, 16:30-17:00)
    (free jerry t0930 t1000)
    (free jerry t1100 t1130)
    (free jerry t1230 t1300)
    (free jerry t1430 t1500)
    (free jerry t1500 t1530)
    (free jerry t1600 t1630)
  )
  (:goal
    (meeting_scheduled)
  )
)
</problem>

  )

    :precondition (and
      (next ?ts ?tm)
      (next ?tm ?te)
      (free michelle ?ts ?tm)
      (free michelle ?tm ?te)
      (free steven ?ts ?tm)
      (free steven ?tm ?te)
      (free jerry ?ts ?tm)
      (free jerry ?tm ?te)

(define (problem schedule_monday_meeting)
  (:domain meeting_scheduler)
  (:objects
    t0900 t0930 t1000 t1030 t1100 t1130 t1200 t1230 t1300 t1330 t1400 t1430 t1500 t1530 t1600 t1630 t1700 - time
  )
  (:init
    ;; Timeline definition (30-minute intervals)
    (next t0900 t0930)
    (next t0930 t1000)
    (next t1000 t1030)
    (next t1030 t1100)
    (next t1100 t1130)
    (next t1130 t1200)
    (next t1200 t1230)
    (next t1230 t1300)
    (next t1300 t1330)
    (next t1330 t1400)
    (next t1400 t1430)
    (next t1430 t1500)
    (next t1500 t1530)
    (next t1530 t1600)
    (next t1600 t1630)
    (next t1630 t1700)

    ;; Michelle availability (Blocked 11:00-12:00)
    (free michelle t0900 t0930)
    (free michelle t0930 t1000)
    (free michelle t1000 t1030)
    (free michelle t1030 t1100)
    (free michelle t1200 t1230)
    (free michelle t1230 t1300)

To schedule the meeting for Raymond, Billy, and Donald according to their constraints and preferences, follow this plan:

**Step 1: Schedule the meeting from 9:30 to 10:00.**

This 30-minute timeslot satisfies all individual schedules:
*   **Raymond** is available (his first conflict ends at 9:30 and his next begins at 11:30).
*   **Billy** is available (his first conflict starts at 10:00, and this time respects his preference to avoid meetings after 15:00).
*   **Donald** is available (his first conflict ends at 9:30 and his next begins at 10:00).

------------------------------

TASK: You need to schedule a meeting for Roy, Kathryn and Amy for half an hour between the work hours of 9:00 to 17:00 on Monday. 

Here are the existing schedules for everyone during the day: 
Roy has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 10:00 to 10:30, 11:00 to 11:30, 12:30 to 13:00; 
Kathryn has meetings on Monday during 9:30 to 10:00, 16:30 to 17:00; 
Amy has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:00 to 14:30, 15:00 to 16:00, 16:30 to 17:00; 

Amy would rather not meet on Monday after 15:30. Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints.

*   **Kathryn** is free (her blocked slots are 9:30-10:00 and 16:30-17:00).
*   **Amy** is free (her blocked slots are 9:00-14:30, 15:00-16:00, and 16:30-17:00).

You are an expert at scheduling meetings. You are given a few constraints on the existing schedule of each participant, the meeting duration, and possibly some preferences on the meeting time. Note there exists a solution that works with existing schedule of every participant. Here are a few example tasks and solutions:

TASK: You need to schedule a meeting for Arthur, Michael and Samantha for one hour between the work hours of 9:00 to 17:00 on Monday. 

Here are the existing schedules for everyone during the day: 
Arthur is busy on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 10:30 to 12:00, 16:00 to 17:00; 
Michael has meetings on Monday during 13:00 to 13:30, 14:00 to 14:30; 
Samantha has blocked their calendar on Monday during 10:30 to 11:00, 12:00 to 15:00, 15:30 to 17:00; 

Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints.

**Step 1: Schedule Meeting (09:30 - 10:30)**
Schedule a one-hour meeting for Arthur, Michael, and Samantha starting at **09:30** and ending at **10:30**.

Charlotte do not want to meet on Monday after 13:30. Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints.

To schedule the meeting for Kathryn, Charlotte, and Lauren while adhering to all personal constraints and availability, follow this step:

**Step 1: Schedule the meeting from 10:00 to 10:30.**

This 30-minute timeslot on Monday is the selected time for the meeting as it ensures all participants are available:
- **Kathryn** is free (her next block starts at 10:30).
- **Charlotte** is free and this occurs before her 13:30 cutoff.
- **Lauren** is free (her previous block ended at 10:00).

Dylan has blocked their calendar on Monday during 10:30 to 11:00, 13:30 to 14:00; 
Jerry is busy on Monday during 9:00 to 11:00, 11:30 to 14:30, 16:00 to 17:00; 

Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_2
------------------------------
To schedule a one-hour meeting for Thomas, Dylan, and Jerry that satisfies all their availability constraints, follow this plan:

1.  **Schedule a 1-hour meeting from 14:30 to 15:30.**
    *   **Thomas** is available as his calendar is open all day.
    *   **Dylan** is available as his afternoon conflicts end at 14:00.
    *   **Jerry** is available as his midday conflict ends at 14:30 and his next conflict does not begin until 16:00.

This action satisfies the goal of finding a time slot that works for all participants' schedules.
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an expert at scheduling meetings. You are given a few constraints on the existing schedule of each participant, the meeting duration, and possibly some preferences on the meeting time. Note there exists a solution that works with existing schedule of every participant. Here are a few example tasks and solutions:

TASK: You need to schedule a meeting for Heather, Nicholas and Zachary for half an hour between the work hours of 9:00 to 17:00 on Monday. 

Here are the existing schedules for everyone during the day: 
Heather has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 10:30 to 11:00, 13:00 to 14:00, 14:30 to 15:00, 16:00 to 16:30; 
Nicholashas no meetings the whole day.
Zachary has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:00 to 10:30, 11:00 to 12:00, 12:30 to 13:00, 13:30 to 16:30; 

Zachary would rather not meet on Monday after 14:00. Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints.

Based on the provided PDDL plan and specification, here is the scheduled meeting:

**Step 1: Schedule the meeting from 12:00 to 12:30 on Monday.**

This meeting involves Heather, Nicholas, and Zachary. It lasts for 30 minutes and satisfies all constraints:
*   **Heather** is available as she has no conflicting meetings between 11:00 and 13:00.
*   **Nicholas** is available as he has no meetings scheduled for the entire day.
*   **Zachary** is available during this specific window (he is blocked from 11:00–12:00 and 12:30–13:00).
*   **Preferences:** This time slot respects Zachary's preference to meet before 14:00.

PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an expert at scheduling meetings. You are given a few constraints on the existing schedule of each participant, the meeting duration, and possibly some preferences on the meeting time. Note there exists a solution that works with existing schedule of every participant. Here are a few example tasks and solutions:

TASK: You need to schedule a meeting for Adam, Jerry and Matthew for half an hour between the work hours of 9:00 to 17:00 on Monday. 

Here are the existing schedules for everyone during the day: 
Adam has meetings on Monday during 10:00 to 10:30, 12:30 to 13:00, 13:30 to 14:30; 
Jerry has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 12:00 to 12:30, 15:00 to 16:00; 
Matthew has meetings on Monday during 9:30 to 11:00, 11:30 to 12:30, 13:00 to 14:00, 14:30 to 17:00; 

Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To schedule a meeting that accommodates everyone's existing commitments, follow this instruction:

**Step 1: Schedule the meeting for Adam, Jerry, and Matthew on Monday from 11:00 to 11:30.**

This 30-minute time slot is the only period between 9:00 and 17:00 where all three participants are simultaneously available:
- **Adam** is free (his next meeting isn't until 12:30).
- **Jerry** is free (his previous block ended at 9:30 and his next isn't until 12:00).
- **Matthew** is free (his morning meeting ends at 11:00 and his next one starts at 11:30).
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an expert at scheduling meetings. You are given a few constraints on the existing schedule of each participant, the meeting duration, and possibly some preferences on the meeting time. Note there exists a solution that works with existing schedule of every participant. Here are a few example tasks and solutions:

TASK: You need to schedule a meeting for Diane, Kelly and Deborah for half an hour between the work hours of 9:00 to 17:00 on Monday. 

Here are the existing schedules for everyone during the day: 
Diane has meetings on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 12:00 to 12:30, 14:30 to 15:30; 
Kelly has meetings on Monday during 9:30 to 10:00, 10:30 to 11:00; 
Deborah is busy on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 10:00 to 14:00, 14:30 to 17:00; 

Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints. 
SOLUTION:

1. **Schedule the Meeting**: Arrange a 30-minute meeting for Diane, Kelly, and Deborah on Monday from **14:00 to 14:30**.

This time slot satisfies all constraints:

*   **Deborah** is available (her mid-day conflict ends at 14:00 and her late afternoon conflict begins at 14:30).

Diana has blocked their calendar on Monday during 11:30 to 12:00, 13:00 to 13:30;

Janet is busy on Monday during 9:00 to 10:00, 12:30 to 13:00, 14:00 to 15:00, 15:30 to 17:00; 

Janet do not want to meet on Monday after 12:00. Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints.

**Step 1: Schedule the meeting**
Schedule a 30-minute meeting for Alexander, Elizabeth, and Walter on Monday from **15:00 to 15:30**.

This time slot works for all participants because:
*   **Alexander** is free (his busy period ends at 15:00 and another starts at 16:00).
*   **Elizabeth** is free (her busy period ends at 14:30).
*   **Walter** is free (his busy period ends at 14:30 and another starts at 15:30).

TASK: You need to schedule a meeting for Gerald, Roy and Barbara for half an hour between the work hours of 9:00 to 17:00 on Monday.

Gerald is busy on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 13:00 to 14:00, 15:00 to 15:30, 16:00 to 17:00; 
Roy is free the entire day.
Barbara has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:30 to 10:00, 11:30 to 14:00, 14:30 to 15:00, 15:30 to 17:00; 

Gerald would like to avoid more meetings on Monday before 13:00. Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints.
